Check that we are not exceeding available to send now: Tarragona has 2 archers available, ok. Zaragoza has 2 archers available, sending 1 ok. Huesca has 3 archers available, sending 3 ok.

Now builds:

We'll decide builds as earlier:

- Barcelona: build 2 archers
- Tarragona: build 2 archers
- Girona: build 2 archers
- Castellon: build 2 archers
- Zaragoza: build 2 archers
- Huesca: build 2 archers (maybe)
- Pamplona: build 2 archers

But we need to check resource limits for each.

Barcelona: wood50, iron60 -> can build 2 archers (40w,20i) ok.

Tarragona: wood50, iron50 -> 2 archers ok.

Girona: wood50, iron50 -> 2 archers ok.

Castellon: wood50, iron60 -> 2 archers ok.

Zaragoza: wood50, iron60 -> 2 archers ok.

Huesca: wood60, iron50 -> 2 archers cost 40w,20i leaves wood20, iron30 ok.

Pamplona: wood40, iron30 -> 2 archers cost 40w,20i leaves wood0, iron10 ok.

Thus all can build 2 archers.

We could also consider building some spearmen for defense, but archers are fine.

Now we need to ensure we don't exceed troop cap (300) - far.

Plan: note to next turn: we will attack Valencia and Burgos with archer-heavy armies, reinforce defenses, continue building archers, aim to capture more villages.

Statement: what we did this turn: we sent archer reinforcements to Castellon and Pamplona, built archers in all villages, preparing to take neutral villages Valencia and Burgos next turn.
